- **What held:** "much better with friends", between 7.1 and 11.6 per 100 in every period.
- **Outside events came late.** The user-agreement clause, AI art (2026-05 to 2026-09) and the console
  version left behind are all 2025-26 events.
- **Events the reviewers tie turns to** (from reviews, not patch notes): bots added in a patch won back a
  player who had given up on solo; reconnecting added in a patch flipped one review to a thumbs up; the
  move to studio servers made lag worse, say 3 reviewers.
- The page's lesson: a game read only in its first year would miss all three late clusters.

### Co-op and online play
- **Friends carry the game.** "Much better with friends" 181 times; 10 reviews play with family; it
  rewards teamwork, 8 times. Needs a group, 5 times.
- **It also works alone.** Works solo 56 times; too hard alone 14.
- **Dropping out and rejoining: 8 reviews** (7 cannot rejoin, 1 a disconnect loses the run). The second
  most helpful thumbs-down review (71 helpful): *"Absolutely disgusting that you can't reconnect to your
  own game session if someone DC's"* (168616519). One review flipped to a thumbs up when patch notes said
  reconnecting had been added (171154399).
- **Joining a run already under way: 3 reviews** ask for it, one naming Deep Rock Galactic as the model
  (179561786, 13 helpful).
- **Hosting: 3 reviews** say lag got worse after the studio moved sessions off players' machines to its
  own servers (173877091, 174405754, 178523682). This is the reviewers' account; no patch notes were
  pulled. One more says there is no ping shown before you join, so a bad session is found only after the
  run starts.
- **Lag and out-of-sync play:** 12 reviews.
- **Scaling to the number of players:** praised 2 times; on higher settings the extra difficulty per
  player grows faster than a full group can handle, 3 times; one strong player can carry the rest, 4
  times; more players makes it trivial, 1.
- **Death in co-op:** a dead player has to watch until the next checkpoint, 2 times.
- **Loot per player is liked:** 4 reviews say nobody fights over drops; you can share almost everything
  except cash (223278780).
- **Same hero:** one review likes that several friends may pick the same hero.
- **Bots:** said 8 times in 6 reviews. One player had written off solo play because solo bosses were
  *"two hit deaths"*, until optional bots came in a patch (192796690). The cost is named too: bots deal
  about an eighth of your damage while enemy health scales up (234049844, the reviewer says).
- **Strangers and matchmaking:** 23 reviews on too few players. *"your game is dying cause of low player
  base"*, with a plea for deep discounts to bring people in (216633728). *"matchmaking worse than tarkov,
  not worth playing if no friends"* (203253379).
- **Locked mode:** a mode opens only at a set level, 2 times (so a new player may not join friends at
  once).
- **Crossplay:** 1 review says crossplay skips the store they bought it on.
- **Friendly fire:** nothing said (0 remarks).

### Combat, movement and feel
- **Combat hits hard** (69 times) and **you get to feel overpowered** (48 times, rising over the years).
  The page calls breaking the game the point.
- **Movement is the one thing about feel that keeps getting worse in the reviews.** Slow movement 15
  reviews and missing modern moves 9, together the most repeated complaint about how the game feels.
  Responsive movement is praised 5 times. *"You can jump and dash, that is it, not even a sprint"*
  (230540835). *"you move like a snail with a pathetically short dash move"*, with no safety window on
  the dash (204410221). One reviewer (230540835) adds that the slow movement is what keeps fairly simple
  enemies dangerous.
- **Weak-spot hits that do not count** as critical hits: 2 reviews. **Melee hits that do not land:** 1.
- **Balance:** every option plays differently 24 times; one option beats the rest 10 reviews; some
  options are never worth taking 9; something should be weakened 7; one playstyle has too few tools 3;
  upgrades that come with a cost, so each is a real choice, praised 2 times.
- **Each hero plays its own way** - 48 times.
- **Controls are almost silent:** a controller that does not work 1, a missing key binding 1, a wish for
  aim assist 1.
- **Camera:** first person, so the pages say nothing about a third-person camera.

### Enemies, bosses and difficulty
- **Hard in a good way** 48 times (but falling, from 3.5 to 0.5 per 100); **difficulty levels well
  spaced** 24 times.
- **One-hit deaths at the top difficulty:** 10 reviews, rising from 0.0 to 1.2 per 100 in 2025-26.
- **Too easy:** 7 reviews. Players also say their power outgrows the challenge late in a run, so late
  play stops needing thought (no count given).
- **Enemy variety:** praised 14 times, lacking 8 reviews.
- **Bosses:** a highlight for 14. Solo bosses were *"two hit deaths"* for one player until bots came
  (192796690). Bosses that hide behind phases where they cannot be hurt: 1 or 2.
- **Unfair enemies, single remarks** (1 or 2 each): enemies that cannot be countered, enemies that soak
  too many bullets, attacks poorly signalled before they land.

### Progression, loot, randomness and grind
- **Inside a run:** builds deep and varied 110 times; a guaranteed hero upgrade at the end of every floor
  is praised as the best pacing in the genre (235815154); satisfying progress 55; upgrades that come at
  a price, so each pick is a real choice, 2.
- **Between runs:** the slow start is 22 reviews (highest in the corpus) and grindy 18. The page notes
  that the between-run upgrades that make early runs weak are the same ones players praise later. The
  cross-game page says Warframe has the same pattern: both games put the long-term progress before the
  fun build, and both are praised for the build once it arrives.
- **Randomness:** keeps runs fresh 47 times; the thing you need may never show up 15 reviews; luck decides
  the outcome 12 reviews.
- **Choice at the start:** 2 reviews want to choose their starting weapon; 1 wants to start a run from a
  later stage instead of replaying the opening each time.
- **Seasons:** 6 reviews praise that old seasons never expire and their rewards can be bought later with
  earned currency - *"No FOMO"* (220125902).
- **Paid heroes:** 24 call the DLC fair; 10 reviews say paid heroes are stronger or too much is paid.
- **Lost progress:** progress not saved, 5 times.

### Runs, content and replay value
- **It keeps pulling you back** - 106 times, though this fell from 7.9 to 3.6 per 100 over the years.
  **"I want more because it is good"** - 42 times.
- **A game to relax to** - 30 times.
- **Repetitive** - 17 reviews. **Too little content** - 16 reviews.
- **No mod support** - 10 reviews.
- **Every run starts at the first stage** - 1 review wants to begin further in.
- The pages do not give run length or count levels.

### Money and price
- **The price is fair** - 100 times (5.3 per 100), but this fell from 9.7 per 100 in Early Access to 3.8
  in 2025-26.
- **The DLC is fair** - 24 times, rising from 0.2 to 3.8 per 100 over the same years.
- **Only worth buying on sale** - 8 reviews.
- **Paid heroes:** 10 reviews say DLC heroes are stronger or too much of the roster costs extra (some
  call it power creep, or pay that affects play, or content behind a second purchase); 1 says full
  completion needs paid DLC (216038763).
- **Seasons:** old season rewards can be bought later with currency earned by playing (6 reviews praise
  it).
- One reviewer asks for deep discounts to bring players back (216633728).

### Tech: performance, crashes, bugs
- Engineering is only 3.8% of everything said (128 times).
- **Good:** bugs rare and minor 13; well optimised 9; runs well on modest hardware 8.
- **Bad:** lag and out-of-sync play 12 reviews; worse lag after the move to studio servers 3; cannot
  rejoin or a disconnect loses the run 8; progress not saved 5; stutter 4; unstable frame rate 3; needs
  an internet connection 3; crashes again and again 1; the menus lag 1; no ping shown before joining 1.
- The engine is not named.

### The studio, updates and community
- **Updates keep coming:** 73 times, the highest rate in the corpus. **A patch made it better:** 29.
  **The developers listen and change things:** 15.
- **Each update brings new badly translated text** (translation complaints 7 in Early Access, 20 in
  2025-26).
- **A console version left without updates** (kept without the seasons and DLC): 6 reviews.
- **AI-made art:** 5 thumbs-down reviews, all 2026-05 to 2026-09. The only other game in the corpus where
  this came up is ARC Raiders, with 1.
- **A user-agreement clause:** 1 review, 91 helpful, the most helpful thumbs-down review in the sample
  (207418062).
- **The studio treats its staff badly** (the reviewer says): 2 reviews.
- **Community:** 181 of the 246 community remarks are about playing with friends. Story is almost never
  mentioned (9 times in all); nobody comes for a story. Sound is 17 times (one fight far louder than the
  rest, 1); accessibility 3.

### What players asked for
- **Better English text:** 36 reviews on bad wording, 1 on text left in Chinese.
- **Reconnect after a drop:** 8 reviews (added later, a reviewer says).
- **Join a run in progress:** 3 reviews, one naming Deep Rock Galactic.
- **Faster movement or modern moves** (sprint, a longer dash, a dash with a safety window): 15 + 9 reviews
  on movement.
- **Mod support:** 10 reviews.
- **More content:** too little 16 reviews; "I want more because it is good" 42 times.
- **More enemy variety:** 8 reviews.
- **More players / easier matchmaking:** 23 reviews; one asks for deep discounts to get there.
- **Console updates:** 6 reviews.
- **Choose the starting weapon:** 2 reviews. **Start a run from a later stage:** 1.
- **More tools for one playstyle:** 3 reviews.
- **Bots for solo** (asked for and then added, say reviewers): said 8 times in 6 reviews.
- **See the ping before joining:** 1. **Aim assist:** 1. **Crossplay on the reviewer's store:** 1.

### Only in this game
- **When the page was first written (2026-09-28), 22 of the 357 kinds of remark used here appeared in no
  other game**; 26 new kinds were built while reading it, and 11 of the 22 were seen only once. The most
  used at that time were: a console version left behind while the PC version is updated (6 reviews); the
  odd translated wording is charming (3); one playstyle has too few tools to build around (3); you cannot
  choose your starting weapon (2); weak-spot hits do not count as critical hits (2); the studio treats its
  staff badly (2).
- **Corrected in the rebuild (2026-10-03): a fresh script run lists 13 kinds used here and in no other
  game**; the rest have since turned up in games read later. The 13: the studio treats its staff badly
  (2); upgrades come with a cost (2); cannot choose the starting weapon (2); a mode locked behind a level
  (2); weak-spot hits do not count (2); cannot start from a later stage (1); everyone can play the same
  character (1); full completion needs paid DLC (1); wants aim assist (1); melee hits that do not land
  (1); the menus lag (1); one fight far louder than the rest (1); no gore is a plus (1).
- **Translation as a top complaint** - ten times the rate of any other game read at size.
- **The highest rate in the corpus** for steady updates (3.9 per 100) and for the slow start (1.2 per 100).
- **Very short reviews:** mean 32.5 words, and 1,132 of 1,878 (60%) are 15 words or fewer, which is why
  so many remarks are a bare thumbs up (728 times). That also capped how many new kinds of remark each
  batch could build: 26 in 38 batches, about 0.7 a batch, against about 2 for Escape from Duckov and 2.4
  for Warframe.
- **Hours:** 292 reviews show 100 hours or more, and 2 show 1,000 or more.

### For Dominion - our reading
1. **Any upgrade text players read in the middle of a run must be checked at every update, not once.**
   Evidence: bad English is the top complaint, 36 reviews (1.9 per 100, ten times any other game read at
   size), and it grew from 7 reviews in Early Access to 20 in 2025-26 as each season added new text.
   One reviewer adds that item text runs *"at least 3 lines long"* (230540835). The cross-game page draws this same lesson.
   Dominion could: keep upgrade text short (one line where possible), use icons and numbers, and make a
   text check part of every patch, in every language it ships. Strength: **medium** (clear and rising,
   but tied to this game's translation).
2. **Build for friends first, and make solo work too.** Evidence: "much better with friends" 181 times
   (fourth highest in the corpus); works alone 56 times against too hard alone 14. Dominion could: make
   the four-player session easy to start with friends, and tune and test a real solo path (with scaling
   or bots). Strength: **strong**.
3. **Let a player who drops get back into the run.** Evidence: 8 reviews; the second most helpful
   thumbs-down review (71 helpful) is about not being able to reconnect (168616519); one review turned
   to a thumbs up when reconnecting was added (171154399). Dominion could: on a listen server, let a
   dropped client rejoin the same run with their build kept, and decide what happens when the host drops
   (keep the run alive, save it, or hand it over). Strength: **medium**.
4. **Allow joining a run in progress.** Evidence: 3 reviews, one naming Deep Rock Galactic (179561786, 13
   helpful). Dominion could: let friends drop into a running session, at least between rooms or arenas.
   Strength: **weak**.
5. **Hosting changes are felt.** Evidence: 3 reviews say lag got worse after the move to studio servers;
   lag and out-of-sync play 12 reviews; 1 more says no ping is shown before you join, so a bad session is
   found only after the run starts. Dominion could: since it hosts on a player's machine, test play on
   weak home connections and show the host's connection quality before a run. Strength: **weak**.
6. **Movement needs a sprint and a real dodge, or players will feel slow.** Evidence: slow movement 15
   and missing modern moves 9; 14 of the 22 reviews on movement are from 2025-26; missing modern moves
   rose from 0.0 to 2.1 per 100; *"not even a sprint"* (230540835); a dash with no safety window
   (204410221). The counterpoint, from one reviewer: slow movement is what keeps simple enemies
   dangerous. Dominion could: give a sprint and a dodge with a short safety window, and keep enemies
   threatening by how they attack, not by keeping the player slow. Strength: **medium**.
7. **A guaranteed upgrade at fixed points keeps a run building.** Evidence: builds deep and varied 110;
   satisfying progress 55; an upgrade at the end of every floor called the best pacing in the genre
   (235815154). Dominion could: give a sure upgrade after each arena or area, on top of random drops.
   Strength: **strong**.
8. **Let players feel overpowered, but guard the top difficulty against one-shot deaths and boredom.**
   Evidence: feeling overpowered 48 times, rising from 1.1 to 4.8 per 100; over the same years "hard in a
   good way" fell from 3.5 to 0.5 per 100; one-hit deaths 10 reviews, rising from 0.0 to 1.2 per 100;
   too easy 7; power outgrowing the challenge also named. Dominion could: let builds get strong, raise
   challenge with enemy count and behaviour rather than one-shot damage, and flag one-shot attacks
   clearly. Strength: **medium**.
9. **Do not make the first runs feel weak because of between-run upgrades.** Evidence: slow start 22
   reviews (highest in the corpus, just ahead of Warframe's 1.1 per 100, which has the same pattern) and
   grindy 18. Dominion could: make the first runs fun on their own, with meta upgrades adding options
   more than raw power. Strength: **medium**.
10. **Randomness is liked until the needed piece never comes.** Evidence: keeps runs fresh 47 times;
    the thing you need never shows up 15 reviews; luck decides the outcome 12 reviews. Dominion could:
    add bad-luck protection or a way to steer rolls (rerolls, a shop, a choice of three). Strength:
    **medium**.
11. **Bots can save solo play, but only if their damage keeps up.** Evidence: said 8 times in 6 reviews;
    "an absolute godsend" (235865471); won back a player who found solo bosses too deadly (192796690);
    but bots deal about an eighth of your damage while enemy health scales (234049844). Dominion could:
    if it adds bots, scale enemies to the humans, not the bots. Strength: **weak**.
12. **Loot each player can keep avoids fights inside the team.** Evidence: 4 reviews praise that nobody
    fights over drops (223278780). Dominion could: give each player their own drops, or share them in a
    clear way. Strength: **weak**.
13. **A small player base kills matchmaking with strangers.** Evidence: 23 reviews on too few players,
    rising from 2 to 10 per period. Dominion could: lean on friend invites and solo play, and not build
    features that need a full public lobby to work. Strength: **medium**.
14. **Seasons that never expire are praised.** Evidence: 6 reviews; *"No FOMO"* (220125902, 545 hours).
    Dominion could: if it runs seasons, keep old ones playable and their rewards earnable later.
    Strength: **weak** (few reviews, but no pushback at all).
15. **Paid heroes that are stronger cost trust.** Evidence: DLC fair 24 times, but 10 reviews say paid
    heroes are stronger or too much is paid; 1 complains that full completion needs paid DLC. Dominion
    could: keep paid content side-grades, not upgrades, and keep achievements reachable in the base game.
    Strength: **weak to medium**.
16. **Trust can be lost outside the game.** Evidence: a user-agreement clause (1 review, 91 helpful, the
    top thumbs-down review); AI-made art (5 thumbs-down reviews, 2026-05 to 2026-09); a console version
    left behind (6 reviews). Dominion could: keep the user agreement plain, say how art is made, and keep
    every platform it ships on updated together. Strength: **medium** (small counts, but high in
    helpful votes and all recent).
17. **A steady stream of updates is the strongest praise for a live game.** Evidence: 73 times, the
    highest rate in the corpus; a patch made it better 29; developers listen 15. Dominion could: plan a
    regular update rhythm before launch. Strength: **strong**.
18. **Watch the complaints over years, not just at launch.** Evidence: complaints went from 20.9 to 46.3
    per 100 and the thumb from 97.9% to 88.8% over six years, while praise held. Dominion could: track
    review complaints by period after launch, so a slow slide shows up early. Strength: **medium**.
19. **A one-line pitch that names other games helps players explain it.** Evidence: explained by naming
    other games 147 times; 114 name Borderlands, 57 Risk of Rain; "Borderlands as a roguelike". Dominion
    could: pick its own "X meets Y" line and use it in marketing. Strength: **medium**.
20. **A memorable cast helps.** Evidence: appealing heroes 79 times; each plays its own way 48. Dominion
    could: make each playable character look and play clearly different. Strength: **medium**.
21. **Weak-spot hits must count.** Evidence: 2 reviews say head or weak-spot hits do not register as
    critical; 1 more says melee hits do not land. Dominion could: test weak-spot and melee hit boxes
    against the art. Strength: **weak**.
22. **Story is not why people play this kind of game.** Evidence: story is mentioned 9 times in 3,350
    remarks. Dominion could: keep story light inside runs and spend effort on the loop. Strength: **weak**
    (absence of talk, not a complaint).
23. **Mod support is asked for.** Evidence: 10 reviews. Dominion could: decide early whether to allow
    mods. Strength: **weak**.
24. **Scale enemies to the group, and test a full group of four on the hardest setting.** Evidence:
    scaling praised 2 times, but on higher settings the extra difficulty per player outgrows a full
    group, 3 times; one strong player can carry the rest, 4 times; more players makes it trivial, 1.
    Dominion could: test every difficulty with one, two, three and four players, and make sure a full
    team is never worse off than a smaller one. Strength: **weak**.
25. **Give a dead teammate something to do, or a way back before the next checkpoint.** Evidence: a dead
    player has to watch until the next checkpoint, 2 times. Dominion could: offer revives, a respawn
    timer or a task for the fallen player during an arena or extraction. Strength: **weak**.
26. **Do not lock co-op with friends behind a level.** Evidence: a mode locked until a set level, 2 times.
    Dominion could: let a new player join friends in their first session. Strength: **weak**.
27. **Run progress must not be lost.** Evidence: progress not saved, 5 times; a disconnect loses the run,
    1. Dominion could: save run state often, on the host, so a crash or drop does not wipe a run.
    Strength: **weak**.
28. **"Fair price" praise wears off as a game ages; paid extras get judged instead.** Evidence: fair-price
    praise fell from 9.7 per 100 in Early Access to 3.8 in 2025-26, while "the DLC is fair" rose from 0.2
    to 3.8. Dominion could: expect the launch price to be praised early and the DLC to be judged later,
    and plan DLC that players will call fair. Strength: **weak to medium** (clear trend, but per-period
    margins of ±4 to ±5).
29. **The upgrade-per-pick should be a real choice.** Evidence: upgrades that come with a cost, praised 2
    times (only in this game); some options never worth taking 9 reviews; one option beats the rest 10.
    Dominion could: give upgrades trade-offs so no pick is a free win, and check pick rates to find the
    dead and the dominant ones. Strength: **weak**.
30. **Let players skip the part of a run they have done many times.** Evidence: 1 review wants to start
    from a later stage; repetitive 17 reviews. Dominion could: consider unlockable later starting points
    for practised players. Strength: **weak**.

### Limits
- A 4.3% sample; whole-sample shares carry ±2.51%, and each of the four periods about ±4 to ±5.
- The first month (2020-05) carries the heaviest weight (×45), because Steam returned only 10 of the 20
  reviews wanted. Every month is read at 10 to 115 reviews, so a single month is direction only.
- 154 reviews (8.2%) were edited later and are counted on the day they were written; how to treat edits
  is open with Rico.
- No patch notes were pulled. The server move, the reconnect fix, the bots patch, the user-agreement
  clause, the AI art, the console claims and the claim about how the studio treats its staff all come
  from reviews and were not checked.
- Hours are those shown on the review. **The thumbs-down median was first given as 13 hours; a later
  count from the summary files gives 12.** The page keeps both rather than guess; this entry now does
  the same.
- **Corrected in the rebuild:** the count of kinds of remark found only in this game was 22 when first
  written and is 13 on the fresh run (2026-10-03), because games read later used the rest.
- Early batches and later batches were read in different sessions; the page cannot rule out that the
  late reader named movement and population complaints more often.
- Reviews are very short (60% are 15 words or fewer); 773 remarks (23.1%) said nothing specific.
- 24 reviews were written in another language; a search today finds 12 carrying that mark, and the page
  does not record how the 24 were counted. About 60,000 reviews in other languages were not read.
- Rows added in this pass with counts of "times" (rather than "reviews") come from the page's count of
  remarks per kind, not reviews; one review can say a thing more than once.

---

## Escape from Duckov

**Read:** 1,166 of 11,004 English reviews (10.6%), ±2.83%, written 2025-10 to 2026-09. **90.9% thumbs up**
in the sample (1,055 of 1,160; Steam, all languages: 84%, *Very Positive*, on 104,492 reviews, 2026-09-27).
**Pages:** `findings/escape-from-duckov-english.md`, `findings/escape-from-duckov.md`, `findings/cross-game.md` section 21.

**What it is:** A single-player extraction shooter seen from above (top-down), against the game only, no
other players. It is a comic take on Escape from Tarkov, with ducks. You go out on a run, gather loot,
and must get out alive; if you die you lose your gear, with one chance to go back and pick it up. Areas
the reviews name include Farm Town and the Labs (late game). Difficulty can be set in detail, including
whether you lose all your gear on death. There is a character creator, a base you build up by unlocking
stations, and a Steam Workshop for mods. Paid game. On Steam since 2025-10. The reviews name the studio
as Team Soda and the publisher as Bilibili (not checked against store data). The pages do not name the
engine. There is no co-op except through mods.

**How close to Dominion:** The closest game on either planning list (A1 on the action-roguelike list),
picked under Rico's standing order of 2026-09-25. It shares PvE extraction, runs that start and end,
and loot you can lose. It does not share co-op (it is single-player), the camera (top-down, not third
person), hosting (there is nothing to host), or the sci-fi arena side. So it tells Dominion a lot about
the extraction loop and nothing about playing it together.

### The short version (plain words)
Players judge it against Tarkov, and it wins: 135 of 1,160 reviews name Tarkov, and only 6 of those are
thumbs down. Taking PvP out is read as the feature, not a gap: 51 times, all under a thumbs up, and 16
people who normally avoid the genre say it won them over. Its biggest complaints are wishes from fans:
co-op (39 times, 36 of them thumbs up) and controller support (33 times, and 12 of those 33 are thumbs
down). The thumbs-up share fell from 96.7% to 74.4% in one month (2025-12), and that drop came from a
fight over a workshop mod, not the game. Where the game itself loses people is the end game, where the
part you need is left to chance. In one line, from the rebuilt page: every specific praise near the top
is about what taking the other players out allowed, and the complaints are about what is still missing
without them - someone to play with, and someone to trade you the part.

On the same count used for every large game:

| | Deep Rock Galactic | Risk of Rain 2 | **Escape from Duckov** | Warframe | Helldivers 2 | ARC Raiders |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Thumbs up, sample | 97.1% | 95.6% | **90.9%** | 89.9% | 83.3% | 79.7% |
| Things said per review | 2.12 | 1.43 | **1.92** | 2.01 | 1.79 | 1.56 |
| Praise per 100 reviews | 175.9 | 104.9 | **134.6** | 127.3 | 108.0 | 86.8 |
| Complaints per 100 reviews | 23.7 | 18.0 | **40.4** | 50.9 | 55.5 | 56.7 |
| Praise to complaint | 7.4 : 1 | 5.8 : 1 | **3.3 : 1** | 2.5 : 1 | 1.95 : 1 | 1.53 : 1 |

In all, 1,561 praise remarks and 469 complaints were counted, out of 2,230 things said (1.92 per review).

**Where the talk goes.** Of the 2,230 things said: game design (coming back, progress, no PvP,
difficulty, the asks for co-op and a controller) 43.3%; remarks that said nothing specific or were about
the review 24.2% (432 a bare thumbs up); comparisons and reputation 11.0%; content and care 6.1%;
community 4.0%; price 3.6%; tech 2.4%; story and tone 2.2%; art 1.6%; sound 1.1%; updates 0.3% (6
remarks); translation 0.2%; accessibility 1 remark.

### What players praised, most to least
Counts are times it was said (one review can say several things), out of 1,160 reviews.

1. **It keeps pulling you back** - 98 times (8.4 per 100 reviews). It peaked at 10.5 per 100 in 2025-12.
2. **It beats its rivals** (mostly Tarkov) - 71 times (6.1 per 100), **the highest rate in the corpus**
   (The Anacrusis 5.6, Warframe 2.1). 135 of 1,160 reviews name Tarkov; 6 of them are thumbs down.
   *"Very fun game that takes out the worst part of Tarkov, which is the cheaters"* (216039510).
   Explaining it by naming other games was said 69 more times. This praise fell from 7.1 and 7.2 per 100
   in the launch months to between 3.8 and 4.1 later.
3. **The price is fair** - 68 times (5.9 per 100).
4. **Progress feels satisfying** - 61 times (5.3 per 100).
5. **No PvP is a feature** - 51 times (4.4 per 100), **every one under a thumbs up.** No other game in the
   corpus reaches 1 per 100 on this (Terminull Brigade 0.8; ARC Raiders 0). *"i have never played an
   extraction shooter, mainly because i just dont enjoy PVP."* (211985235, 100 hours). It held steady
   in every period (3.7 to 5.5 per 100).
6. **Hard in a good way** - 49 times (4.2 per 100).
7. **A game you can relax to** - 48 times (4.1 per 100). The same players often say both 6 and 7: the
   difficulty is chosen. This peaked at 7.1 per 100 in 2026-01 to 03.
8. **Plenty of content** - 48 times (4.1 per 100).
9. **Looks like a joke, plays like a real game** - 39 times (3.4 per 100), all thumbs up, and said of no
   other game when it was read. *"Don't let the silly presentation fool you into thinking this is a joke
   game."* (212777542). A curator review with 127 helpful votes ends *"Just because it's with ducks,
   don't expect it to be any more forgiving!"* (214029794).
10. **Mods extend the game** - 36 times (3.1 per 100); 5.3 per 100 in 2025-12, the month of the mod
    dispute.
11. **The stakes are worth the risk** (losing gear makes runs matter) - 33 times (2.8 per 100). *"Loot runs
    are stressful in a good way, deaths hurt, and progression feels earned."* (215425450).
12. **The satire lands** - 31 times (2.7 per 100).
13. **Difficulty levels are well spaced, so you pick your own** - 29 times (2.5 per 100).
14. **Combat hits hard** - 27 times (2.3 per 100).
15. **Made with care** - 21 times (1.8 per 100).
16. **It looks well directed** - 21 times (1.8 per 100).
17. **"I want more because it is good"** - 19 times (1.6 per 100).
18. **Builds are deep and varied** - 19 times (1.6 per 100).
19. **Good in short sittings** - 18 times (1.6 per 100).
20. **The character creator is fun** - 16 times (1.4 per 100); still said of no other game.
21. **It won over someone who avoids the genre** - 16 times (1.4 per 100), all thumbs up; second in the
    corpus only to Remnant II (1.5).
22. **It works alone** - 15 times (1.3 per 100). All of it came in the launch months (2.0 and 1.9 per
    100), and 0.0 after.
23. **Best game of its kind** - 15 times (1.3 per 100).
24. **The music fits** - 13 times (1.1 per 100).
25. **The bosses are a highlight** - 10 times.
26. **Dying costs little and you get back into it fast** - 8 times.
27. **It runs well** - 7 times; **runs well on my platform** - 8; **bugs are rare and minor** - 6 times.
28. **Difficulty can be tuned in detail** - 6 times, said of no other game when it was read.
29. **Controls are responsive and clear** - 4 times.
30. **Enemy behaviour is good** - 3 times. **The camera works well** - 3 times.
31. **Updates:** a steady stream 3, a patch made it better 2, fixes within hours 1.
32. **The line-of-sight view is clever** - 2 times. **Plays offline** - 2 times.

### What players complained about, most to least
Counts are times it was said, out of 1,160 reviews. **255 of the 469 complaints (54.4%) come from reviews
that still recommend the game.** The 105 thumbs-down reviews have a median of 9 hours played as first
counted (a later count from the summary files gives 8; the page keeps both); the median for all reviews
is 19, and 19 for the 1,055 thumbs-up reviews. **Every complaint is small: the top one is 3.4 per 100.**

1. **A mode players expected is missing** - 39 times (3.4 per 100). **36 of the 39 are thumbs up.** 6
   mention PvP; the rest ask for co-op. *"Now if only it had co-op it would be perfect."* (212024443).
   It stays in every period (2.1 to 5.1 per 100).
2. **No controller support** - 33 times (2.8 per 100). **12 of the 33 are thumbs down**, so this one
   costs votes. 9 of the 33 mention the Steam Deck. A workshop mod adds it, and some reviews point to it.
   *"not having official controller support at this point is criminal"* (228527236). It did not fade:
   1.4 per 100 in 2025-11, then 4.5 and 4.7 per 100 in the next two periods.
3. **The part you need may never drop** - 23 times in 21 reviews (2.0 per 100), 4 of the 21 thumbs down.
   **The highest rate in the corpus** (Remnant II 1.7, Warframe 1.2, Risk of Rain 2 0.3).
4. **Managing the inventory is a chore** - 18 times (1.6 per 100); 3.1 per 100 in 2026-01 to 03.
5. (17 times a thumbs-down review said nothing specific.)
6. **The studio went easy on a mod author who did harm** (the reviewers say) - 16 times (1.4 per 100),
   still said of no other game. See the mod dispute below.
7. **The late game turns into a grind** - 14 reviews (1.2 per 100), 4 thumbs down; said of no other game
   when it was read.
8. **Too grindy** - 13 reviews (1.1 per 100), 6 thumbs down.
9. **Quests force how you play** (a named weapon or method) - 9 times (0.8 per 100).
10. **Missing basic conveniences** - 9 times (0.8 per 100).
11. **Repetitive** - 8 times (0.7 per 100).
12. **Mods said to carry harmful code** - 8 times (0.7 per 100).
13. **Dying costs too much** - 7 times (0.6 per 100).
14. **It takes over your life** (a warning) - 7 times (0.6 per 100); 1.0 per 100 in 2025-11, 0.0 from
    2026-01.
15. **The game hides information you need** - 7 times (0.6 per 100); 0.0 at launch, 2.3 and 2.4 per 100
    in 2025-12 and 2026-01 to 03, when players reach the later areas.
16. **A thumbs-down given as a protest**, not about the game - 7 times (0.6 per 100).
17. **The narrow view is a handicap** - 6 times (0.5 per 100).
18. **Most mods are in another language** - 6 times (0.5 per 100).
19. **The studio leans on mods to fill gaps** - 6 times (0.5 per 100); 0.0 in the launch months, then 1.5
    and 2.4 per 100.
20. **The praise is undeserved** - 5 times (0.4 per 100).
21. **One part is far harder than the rest** - 5 times (0.4 per 100).
22. **Each death makes the next try harder** - 5 times (0.4 per 100).
23. **Progress is held back by real-world timers** - 5 times (0.4 per 100).
24. **Only one chance to get your gear back** - 5 reviews (0.4 per 100), 2 thumbs down.
25. **The line-of-sight view hides enemies** - 4 times (0.3 per 100).
26. **Stutter** - 4 times; few, but severe: one thumbs-down review says it stutters in every Farm Town
    boss fight (232131285).
27. **The base building is shallow** (unlock stations and place them on a grid, nothing to design) - 4
    times; only in this game.
28. **Enemy behaviour is poor** - 4 times; **enemies always know where you are** - 2 times.
29. **No choice of view** - 4 times; **the camera gets in the way** - 3 times.
30. **Moving feels slow** - 4 times.
31. **Too high a price for what it is** - 4 times.
32. **Quests ask for what you already did** (a place found or a boss killed before the quest arrived does
    not count, so you do it again) - 3 times; only in this game.
33. **Broken on my platform** - 3 times; **runs poorly on my platform** - 2; **crashes again and again** -
    2.
34. **Too easy** - 2; **too hard** - 2.
35. **The difficulty dial is not fine enough** - 1 review: full gear loss only comes with higher enemy
    damage (214751423).
36. **Single remarks:** no key rebinding; an awkward default control layout; one button does too many
    things; a dodge roll that locks you in; the stamina runs out too fast; the scenery swallows shots that
    should pass; no companion in the solo game (1 asks for one).
37. **The duck theme wore thin** - 1 review (217753377).
38. **A collaboration with Tarkov's makers** - 2 thumbs down on 2026-01-31.

### How it changed over time
The later periods hold 250 reviews between them and carry about ±8.5 to ±8.8 each; read them for
direction. Two months, 2025-10 and 2025-11, hold 8,113 of the 11,004 reviews.

| Period | Reviews read | Thumbs up | Praise per 100 | Complaints per 100 | Mean words per review |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2025-10 (launch) | 294 | 93.2% | 105.4 | 26.5 | 32 |
| 2025-11 | 483 | 96.7% | 96.3 | 22.2 | 29 |
| 2025-12 | 133 | **74.4%** | 102.3 | **91.0** | **71** |
| 2026-01 to 03 | 127 | 83.5% | 103.1 | 63.8 | 52 |
| 2026-04 to 09 | 123 | 88.6% | 70.7 | 43.9 | 33 |

- **2025-12 is the break.** Complaints per 100 quadrupled and reviews more than doubled in length. Part
  of it is the mod dispute (13 of December's 34 thumbs down are about it; the "went easy on the mod
  author" complaint hit 9.0 per 100 that month). Part of it is players reaching the end game.
- **End-game complaints start at zero in the launch month and rise after.** The part you need never
  drops: 0 per 100 in 2025-10, 1.7 in 2025-11, 3.0 in 2025-12, 7.1 in 2026-01 to 03. The late-game grind
  and plain grind are both 0 per 100 at launch and between 1.6 and 3.3 per 100 from 2025-12 on. The game
  hiding information follows the same shape (0.0 at launch, 2.3 to 2.4 later). Players who reach the end
  write later.
- **Dying costs too much** rises to 2.4 per 100 in 2026-04 to 09 (from 0.0 to 0.7 before).
- **Controller support** never fades: 3.4, 1.4, 4.5, 4.7, 3.3 per 100 across the five periods.
- **Praise that fell:** plenty of content 6.5 per 100 at launch, 1.6 to 2.4 later; fair price 8.2 to 2.4;
  hard in a good way 4.8 to 1.6; looks well directed 4.4 at launch, under 2.5 after; beats its rivals
  7.1 to about 4; combat hits hard 3.1 to 0.8; works alone 2.0 and 1.9 in the launch months, 0.0 after.
- **Praise that rose:** well-spaced difficulty 0.3 at launch to 4.7 in 2026-01 to 03; a game to relax to
  peaked at 7.1 in 2026-01 to 03; the satire landing peaked at 6.8 in 2025-12.
- **The thumb recovered most of the way** by mid-2026 (88.6%). Copies of one protest statement kept
  appearing until 2026-07.

### Co-op and online play
- **It is single-player.** There is no co-op except through mods, so the pages cannot say anything about
  co-op play itself.
- **Co-op is the top wish.** A missing mode is the top complaint, 39 times, 36 of them thumbs up, and
  most ask for co-op (6 mention PvP). One more review wants an AI companion in the solo game.
- **Taking PvP out is praised** (51 times, all thumbs up), and **16 genre-avoiders were won over.**
- **What solo costs:** with nobody to trade with, a missing rare part can only be found by running the
  same route again. The page says this makes the random drop harder to accept.
- **The two extraction games ask for each other** (cross-game): in ARC Raiders (PvP plus PvE), 42 of 55
  missing-mode requests ask for PvE; here, most ask for co-op - more players, on the same side. The
  cross-game page reads this as co-op PvE extraction, Dominion's format, sitting where both audiences
  point - while noting it is still only two games.
- Hosting, joining, dropping out, scaling, bots, friendly fire, matchmaking, crossplay, servers, lag: not
  applicable; the game has none. It plays offline (praised 2 times).

### Combat, movement and feel
- **Combat hits hard** - 27 times (falling from 3.1 per 100 at launch to 0.8 later).
- **Controller support is missing** - 33 times, 12 of them thumbs down; 9 mention the Steam Deck.
  Controls are responsive and clear, 4 times. Single remarks: no key rebinding, an awkward default
  layout, one button that does too many things (so the game does the wrong one), and a dodge roll that
  locks you in.
- **Movement:** slow, 4 times; the stamina runs out too fast, 1.
- **Camera and sight:** the narrow view is a handicap (6 times); the game draws only what your character
  can see, and 4 complain that this hides enemies, while 2 call the system clever; no choice of view 4;
  the camera works well 3, gets in the way 3.
- **Shots:** the scenery swallows shots that should pass, 1.
- **Enemy ammo is too random to prepare for** - from one end-game review (212801415).

### Enemies, bosses and difficulty
- **Difficulty is a dial, and the dial is praised.** Hard in a good way 49; a game to relax to 48;
  well-spaced levels 29; tuned in detail 6. Too easy and too hard are 2 each. Players describe choosing
  between a relaxed loot run and a punishing one, and some turn full gear loss on or off (212777542).
- **The dial is not fine enough** for one player: full gear loss only comes with higher enemy damage
  (214751423).
- **One part is far harder than the rest** - 5 times. **Each death makes the next try harder** (a
  stronger enemy appears) - 5 times.
- **An area needs gear it never tells you about** - from one end-game review (212801415). That one review
  was detailed enough to add three new kinds of remark (one chance to recover gear, an area that needs
  unmentioned gear, enemy ammo too random to prepare for).
- **Bosses:** a highlight for 10. One thumbs-down review says it stutters in every Farm Town boss fight
  (232131285).
- **Enemy behaviour:** poor 4, good 3; enemies always know where you are, so position and stealth stop
  mattering, 2.
- A curator: *"Just because it's with ducks, don't expect it to be any more forgiving!"* (214029794, 127
  helpful).

### Progression, loot, randomness and grind
- **Progress feels satisfying** - 61 times. **Builds deep and varied** - 19. **Character creator fun** - 16.
- **The end game is where it sours.** Five complaints describe the same stretch:

  | What players said | Times | Reviews | Thumbs down |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | The late game turns into a grind | 14 | 14 | 4 |
  | Too grindy | 13 | 13 | 6 |
  | The part you need may never drop | 23 | 21 | 4 |
  | One part is far harder than the rest | 5 | - | - |
  | Only one chance to get your gear back | 5 | 5 | 2 |

  The shape is the same: early and middle game praised, then the last area (the Labs, or Farm Town
  before them), where keys and rare parts must be found by chance. *"once you get to Jlabs, it becomes
  considerably less fun and more of a grind"* (212074993, 98 hours, thumbs up). *"Early to mid game is
  fantastic!"* - then the end game (212801415, 94 hours, thumbs down). *"Duckov absolutely tanks the
  endgame"* (217761449, 84 hours, thumbs down, 12 helpful). A 100%-completion review at 171 hours calls
  some things *"a MASSIVE grind for little reward"* (212775495).
- **The complaint is about chance, not length.**
- **Losing gear:** stakes worth the risk 33; quick recovery keeps the flow 8. Against: only one chance to
  recover your gear 5; dying costs too much 7; each death makes the next try harder 5. Players like that
  dying costs something and resent losing everything twice. The page: the recovery run is part of the
  tension; losing it is where the complaints start.
- **Real-world timers** hold progress back - 5 times.
- **Quests force a set weapon or method** - 9 times. **Quests ask for what you already did** - 3 times.
- **The base building is shallow** - 4 times.
- **Inventory management is a chore** - 18 times; **missing basic conveniences** - 9; **the game hides
  information you need** - 7.

### Runs, content and replay value
- **It keeps pulling you back** - 98 times. **Plenty of content** - 48 times (strongest at launch).
- **Good in short sittings** - 18 times. **A game to relax to** - 48 times.
- **"I want more because it is good"** - 19 times.
- **Repetitive** - 8 times. **Takes over your life** (a warning) - 7.
- **Mods extend the game** - 36 times; but 6 say the studio leans on mods to fill gaps, and 6 that most
  mods are in another language.
- The pages do not give run length.

### Money and price
- **The price is fair** - 68 times (5.9 per 100), falling from 8.2 per 100 at launch to 2.4 in 2026-04
  to 09. **Too high for what it is** - 4 times.
- A collaboration with Tarkov's makers drew 2 thumbs down on 2026-01-31 (the reviewers say).
- The pages mention no DLC, shop or season pass.

### Tech: performance, crashes, bugs
- Engineering is only 2.4% of everything said (53 times). **Runs well** 7, **runs well on my platform**
  8, **bugs rare and minor** 6, against **stutter** 4, **broken on my platform** 3, **runs poorly on my
  platform** 2, **crashes again and again** 2.
- Thumbs-down performance reviews are few but severe: a stutter in every Farm Town boss fight
  (232131285).
- **The one tech gap people leave over is controller support** (33 times, 12 thumbs down, 9 Steam Deck).
- One reviewer says their offline play is not counted in their Steam hours.
- The engine is not named.

### The studio, updates and community
- **Updates and live service:** only 6 remarks in all (a steady stream 3, a patch made it better 2, fixes
  within hours 1) - the game is under a year old.
- **The mod dispute** (all claims from reviews, not a checked incident): 28 reviews take part, 17 of them
  thumbs down, 13 of those 17 written in 2025-12. The reviews say a popular workshop mod hid code that
  closed the game for players on its author's blacklist, and that the studio asked the author to remove
  it rather than punishing them. The page calls this a new kind of complaint: not the mod itself, but how
  the studio handled the mod's author. It is the sixth most common complaint.
  - The studio went easy on a harmful mod author - 16 times.
  - Mods said to carry harmful code - 8 times.
  - A thumbs-down given as a protest - 7 times.
  - "The controversy did not change my verdict" - 8 times.
  - "The negative reviews are a review bomb" - 2 times.
  - **10 of the 28 defend the game** or tell readers to ignore it, mostly players who never used mods:
    *"I don't know what the downvotes are about. I play the vanilla game, and I enjoy it."* (215970764,
    40 helpful). The dispute review with the most hours is a protest at 233 hours: *"the publisher
    allowing a malicious mod to remain hosted on Steam despite multiple reports is unacceptable."*
    (213339351, 17 helpful).
  - The same statement was pasted at least four times (213987337, 217133568, 226071171, 231744444),
    from 2025-12 to 2026-07.
- **A second, smaller dispute:** a collaboration with Tarkov's makers, 2 thumbs down on 2026-01-31.
- **The tone and satire carry the marketing:** looks like a joke, plays like a real game 39; the satire
  lands 31; the character creator 16. Only one review (217753377) says the duck theme wore thin. Music
  fits the game 13.

### What players asked for
- **Co-op** - most of the 39 missing-mode requests (6 of the 39 mention PvP). **An AI companion in the
  solo game** - 1.
- **Controller support** - 33 times (9 mention the Steam Deck). **Key rebinding** - 1.
- **Better inventory management** - 18 times; **basic conveniences** - 9; **show the information needed**
  - 7.
- **Less chance in the end game** - the part you need never drops, 23 times; late-game grind 14.
- **More than one chance to get gear back** - 5 reviews; less harsh restarts - 7.
- **Quests that let you play your own way** - 9; **quests that count what you already did** - 3.
- **A wider view** - 6; **the line-of-sight view to show more** - 4; **a choice of view** - 4.
- **A deeper base to build** - 4.
- **A finer difficulty dial** (gear loss separate from enemy damage) - 1 review (214751423).
- **Mods in English** - 6.
- **Firmer action against harmful mod authors** - 16.
- **More content** - 19 times as praise ("I want more").

### Only in this game
- **When the page was first written (2026-09-27), 51 of the 289 kinds of remark used here appeared in no
  other game**; 49 new kinds were built while reading it, and 28 of the 51 were seen only once. The most
  used at that time: looks like a joke, plays like a real game (39); the character creator is fun (16);
  the studio went easy on a harmful mod author (16); the late game turns into a grind (14); quests force
  how you play (9); mods said to carry harmful code (8); difficulty can be tuned in detail (6); most mods
  are in another language (6); only one chance to get your gear back (5).
- **Corrected in the rebuild (2026-10-03): a fresh script run lists 35 kinds used here and in no other
  game**; games read later have since used the rest (among them "looks like a joke, plays like a real
  game", the late-game grind and the detailed difficulty dial). The top of the fresh list: the character
  creator is fun 16; went easy on a harmful mod author 16; quests force how you play 9; mods said to carry
  harmful code 8; mods mostly in another language 6; one chance to recover your gear 5; the base
  building is shallow 4; the line-of-sight view hides enemies 4; quests ask for what you already did 3;
  23 more used once or twice.
- **Highest rates in the corpus:** no PvP praised as a feature (4.4 per 100; no other game reaches 1),
  beats its rivals (6.1 per 100), the part you need may never drop (2.0 per 100).
- **The first single-player PvE extraction game in the corpus.**
- **Hours:** 56 reviews show 100 hours or more; none show 1,000 or more.

### For Dominion - our reading
The findings page's own "For Dominion" line: this is the nearest game in the queue so far. What carries
over: taking PvP out is praised as a feature and wins over people who avoid the genre; a difficulty dial
lets one game be both relaxing and hard; the corpse run is liked until it becomes one-try-only; the end
game must not rest on a random drop; and co-op is the first thing fans ask for. The lessons below expand
on each.

1. **PvE-only extraction is a selling point, not a gap - say so loudly.** Evidence: no PvP praised as a
   feature 51 times, all thumbs up (no other game reaches 1 per 100); 16 people who avoid the genre were
   won over, all thumbs up; *"i have never played an extraction shooter, mainly because i just dont enjoy
   PVP."* (211985235). Dominion could: put "no PvP, no cheaters" in its pitch, and aim at players
   who like extraction tension but not other players. Strength: **strong**.
2. **Co-op is the first thing fans of PvE extraction want - Dominion's core is the answer.** Evidence:
   the top complaint, 39 times, 36 of them thumbs up, most asking for co-op; in ARC Raiders, 42 of 55
   missing-mode requests ask for PvE (cross-game). Dominion could: treat four-player co-op extraction as
   the headline, and still keep solo working well, since 15 praise this game as a solo game. Strength:
   **strong**.
3. **A difficulty dial lets one game be both relaxing and hard.** Evidence: hard in a good way 49, a game
   to relax to 48, well-spaced levels 29, tuned in detail 6; too easy and too hard only 2 each; some
   players turn full gear loss on or off (212777542). But one player wanted gear loss separate from enemy
   damage (214751423). Dominion could: offer separate settings for enemy strength and for how much you
   lose on death, chosen by the host for the group. Strength: **strong**.
4. **Losing gear is liked when you can get it back; losing it twice is not.** Evidence: stakes worth the
   risk 33; quick recovery keeps the flow 8; *"Loot runs are stressful in a good way, deaths hurt, and
   progression feels earned."* (215425450). Against: only one chance to recover gear 5, dying costs too
   much 7 (rising late to 2.4 per 100), each death makes the next try harder 5. Dominion could: give a
   recovery run, and think about letting teammates recover a fallen player's gear, so the loss is shared
   and not total. Strength: **medium**.
5. **The end game must not rest on a random drop.** Evidence: the part you need may never drop 23 times
   in 21 reviews (the highest rate in the corpus), rising to 7.1 per 100 in 2026-01 to 03; late-game
   grind 14; grindy 13; *"Duckov absolutely tanks the endgame"* (217761449, 12 helpful). The complaint is
   about chance, not length; solo play makes it worse because nobody can trade the part. Dominion
   could: add a sure path to key items (crafting from parts, a bad-luck counter, or trading between
   teammates in a run). Strength: **medium to strong**.
6. **Ship controller support at launch.** Evidence: 33 times, and 12 of the 33 are thumbs down - the one
   tech gap people leave over; 9 mention the Steam Deck; the request did not fade after launch (4.5 and
   4.7 per 100 in the later periods). Single remarks add no key rebinding, an awkward default layout and
   one button that does too many things. Dominion could: support controllers and the Steam Deck from day
   one, with full rebinding and one action per button by default. Strength: **strong**.
7. **Inventory and menus must not be a chore.** Evidence: inventory is a chore 18; missing basic
   conveniences 9; the game hides information you need 7. Dominion could: sort, stack and auto-move loot
   with one button, and show item values and needs clearly at the extraction screen. Strength: **medium**.
8. **Tell players what gear an area needs, and avoid one sudden spike.** Evidence: one part far harder
   than the rest 5; an area that needs gear it never mentions (212801415); the game hiding information
   rose from 0.0 at launch to 2.3 and 2.4 per 100 once players reached the later areas. Dominion could:
   show threat and gear advice before a run starts. Strength: **weak**.
9. **Do not force a set weapon in quests, and count what players already did.** Evidence: quests force
   how you play 9; quests ask for a place or boss the player already found or killed, and do not count it,
   3. Dominion could: let goals be met in more than one way, and credit goals already met before the
   quest was given. Strength: **weak**.
10. **Real-world timers annoy.** Evidence: 5 times. Dominion could: avoid crafting timers. Strength:
    **weak**.
11. **An outside fight can move the thumb more than the game does - have a mod and moderation policy
    first.** Evidence: thumbs up fell from 96.7% to 74.4% in 2025-12 over a mod dispute; 28 reviews took
    part, 17 thumbs down; the complaint was how the studio handled the mod author (16 times), plus claims
    of harmful code in mods (8). Mods were also praised (36 times), but 6 say the studio leans on mods to
    fill gaps. Dominion could: if it opens mod support, publish clear rules first, act fast and visibly
    on harmful mods, say what was done, and not leave core features (like controller support) to mods.
    Strength: **medium**.
12. **A clear "better than X" story sells a game.** Evidence: beats its rivals 71 (highest in the
    corpus); 135 reviews name Tarkov, 6 thumbs down; *"takes out the worst part of Tarkov, which is the
    cheaters"* (216039510). The cross-game page adds: being measured against a famous rival helps when
    the game is clearly a friendlier version of it. Dominion could: name what it fixes in the games
    players already know. Strength: **medium**.
13. **A light look can hide a serious game - and that surprise is praised.** Evidence: looks like a joke,
    plays like a real game 39, all thumbs up; the satire lands 31; only one review found the theme wore
    thin. Dominion could: if it uses humour, back it with real depth, and show the depth in trailers.
    Strength: **medium**.
14. **A fun character creator is worth having.** Evidence: 16 times, still said of no other game.
    Dominion could: give players a way to make their character their own (looks, colours, a silly
    option). Strength: **weak to medium**.
15. **Runs that fit a short sitting are valued.** Evidence: good in short sittings 18; a game to relax to
    48. Dominion could: keep a standard run short enough for one evening slot, with a clear way out.
    Strength: **medium**.
16. **Watch the camera's view range.** Evidence: the narrow view is a handicap 6; the line-of-sight view
    hides enemies 4 (2 call it clever); no choice of view 4; the camera gets in the way 3, works well 3.
    Dominion could: offer a field-of-view setting and make enemies easy to read at the edge of the
    screen. Strength: **weak** (and from a top-down game).
17. **Boss fights must run smoothly.** Evidence: stutter only 4, but one review says every Farm Town boss
    fight stutters (232131285); runs well 7. Dominion could: test frame rate on boss and arena peaks, on
    the host machine too. Strength: **weak**.
18. **Launch praise for "plenty of content" fades.** Evidence: 6.5 per 100 at launch, 1.6 to 2.4 later;
    fair price fell from 8.2 to 2.4 per 100 over the same months; combat hits hard fell from 3.1 to 0.8.
    Dominion could: plan content updates for the months after launch. Strength: **weak** (late periods
    are small).
19. **When Dominion's own reviews dip, read the words before reading it as a verdict.** Evidence: the
    thumb fell from 96.7% to 74.4% in one month while praise per 100 barely moved (96.3 to 102.3); 13 of
    December's 34 thumbs down were about the mod dispute, and 10 of the 28 dispute reviews defended the
    game. The cross-game page draws the same lesson. Dominion could: when tracking its reviews after
    launch, check what a drop is about before changing the game. Strength: **medium**.
20. **Enemies should have to find you.** Evidence: enemies always know where you are, 2; enemy behaviour
    poor 4 against good 3. Dominion could: make enemies use sight and sound, so position and stealth
    matter on the way to extraction. Strength: **weak**.
21. **A home base needs something to design, not just stations to unlock.** Evidence: the base building
    is shallow, 4 (only in this game). Dominion could: if it has a hub between runs, give players real
    choices in how it looks or works. Strength: **weak**.
22. **Do not let the level geometry eat shots.** Evidence: the scenery swallows shots that should pass,
    1; a dodge roll that locks you in, 1; stamina that runs out too fast, 1. Dominion could: keep
    collision close to what the art shows, and keep dodges short and cancellable. Strength: **weak**
    (single remarks).
23. **Solo players may want a companion.** Evidence: 1 review asks for an AI companion in the solo game;
    "works alone" praise came only in the launch months (2.0 and 1.9 per 100, then 0.0). Dominion could:
    if solo is supported, consider a bot teammate to carry loot or help in fights. Strength: **weak**.
24. **Platform checks matter beyond the controller.** Evidence: broken on my platform 3, runs poorly on
    my platform 2, crashes again and again 2, against runs well on my platform 8; 9 controller requests
    name the Steam Deck. Dominion could: test on the Steam Deck and low-end PCs before launch.
    Strength: **weak**.

### Limits
- A 10.6% sample; whole-sample shares carry ±2.83%. The launch months (294 and 483 reviews read) are
  solid; the three later periods (123 to 133) carry about ±8.5 to ±8.8 and are direction only. 2026-01
  to 2026-09 are read at 20 to 60 reviews a month.
- Two months (2025-10 and 2025-11) hold 8,113 of the 11,004 reviews; the launch month carries the
  heaviest weight (×13.8): Steam returned 294 of the 490 reviews wanted that month, out of 4,060.
- 62 reviews (5.3%) were edited later and are counted on the day they were written; how to treat edits is
  open with Rico.
- No patch notes were pulled. Everything about the mod dispute, the collaboration, the studio and the
  publisher comes from reviews and was not checked.
- Hours are those shown on the review; one reviewer says their offline play is not counted. **The
  thumbs-down median was first given as 9 hours; a later count from the summary files gives 8.** The page
  keeps both; this entry now does the same.
- **Corrected in the rebuild:** the count of kinds of remark found only in this game was 51 when first
  written and is 35 on the fresh run (2026-10-03), because games read later used the rest. Lines above
  that say "said of no other game" now say whether that is still so.
- Reviews are short: mean 38 words, and 679 of 1,160 (59%) are 15 words or fewer; 486 remarks (21.8%)
  said nothing specific. December 2025 is the exception (mean 71 words), lifted by long protest reviews.
- 37 reviews were written partly or wholly in another language, mostly Chinese, and read in translation;
  one slang phrase (211853631) was left untranslated.
- About 93,000 reviews in other languages (much of it Chinese) were not read.
- The game has no co-op, so the pages say nothing about co-op play itself, only the wish for it.
- The game is under a year old; nothing here speaks to a long live service.
- Rows added in this pass that give "times" come from the page's count of remarks per kind, not reviews.

---

## Warframe

**Read:** 3,235 of 302,482 English reviews (1.07%), ±2.16%, written 2013-03 to 2026-09. **89.9% thumbs up**
in the sample (2,908 of 3,233; Steam, all languages: 87%, *Very Positive*, on 684,199 reviews, 2026-09-25).
**Pages:** `findings/warframe-english.md`, `findings/warframe.md`, `findings/cross-game.md` section 20.

**What it is:** A free-to-play co-op looter shooter in third person, from Digital Extremes, on Steam since
2013-03 - thirteen years of live service, the longest in the corpus (163 months). You play missions alone
or with a squad, as what reviewers call a "space ninja", with fast parkour movement. You farm parts to
build new characters (frames) and weapons. Building takes real-world time (crafting timers of 12 hours
to 3 days). Money buys a premium currency (platinum) that can also be traded between players, so a free
player can earn it by selling what they farm. Inventory slots are limited unless you pay or trade.
Clans can build a shared home (a clan hall). Many players follow its story. It is an open-ended loop of
missions, not runs that end. Hosting: 3 reviews say games run peer to peer on a player's connection
rather than on dedicated servers, and 4 say that when the host leaves, everyone's mission ends (the
reviewers' account). The pages do not give the squad size or the engine.

**How close to Dominion:** Close on camera, genre and setting: a third-person sci-fi co-op PvE shooter.
Far on format: there is no run with a start and an end, no extraction, no arena format, and it is
free-to-play with a shop. **Rico stopped this game on 2026-09-24 because it is not a run-based or
extraction game, and resumed it the same week as research; it was later finished.** So read it for the
loop, the grind, the feel and the community, not the format. Its hosting, by the reviewers' account, is
on a player's machine like Dominion's listen server, so the few remarks on the host leaving and on
peer-to-peer play do speak to Dominion (see Co-op).

### The short version (plain words)
The grind is the game, and players know it. "Too grindy" is the top complaint by far (264 times, 8.2 per
100 reviews), flat for thirteen years, and 223 of those 264 reviews (84%) still recommend the game.
Players call the free-to-play model fair (fair price 198 times, everything can be earned 133), because
money mostly buys time, not power (73 times). The pull to come back is praised (221 times) and feared in
the same breath (74 warn it takes over your life, and 60 of those 73 reviews are still thumbs up). The
first hours are the weak spot and always have been: 267 remarks are about how hard the game is to learn.
Tech is not a problem: crashes come up 4 times in 3,233 reviews. The rebuilt page sums it up: the middle
of the loop is what is praised; the game loses people at its two ends - learning it at the start, and
nothing left to chase at the end.

On the same count used for every large game:

| | Deep Rock Galactic | Risk of Rain 2 | **Warframe** | Helldivers 2 | Space Marine 2 | Remnant II | ARC Raiders | Back 4 Blood |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Thumbs up, sample | 97.1% | 95.6% | **89.9%** | 83.3% | 87.6% | 83.6% | 79.7% | 69.2% |
| Things said per review | 2.12 | 1.43 | **2.01** | 1.79 | 1.99 | 2.30 | 1.56 | 2.63 |
| Praise per 100 reviews | 175.9 | 104.9 | **127.3** | 108.0 | 116.0 | 121.6 | 86.8 | 118.0 |
| Complaints per 100 reviews | 23.7 | 18.0 | **50.9** | 55.5 | 61.9 | 77.5 | 56.7 | 137.2 |
| Praise to complaint | 7.4 : 1 | 5.8 : 1 | **2.5 : 1** | 1.95 : 1 | 1.87 : 1 | 1.57 : 1 | 1.53 : 1 | 0.86 : 1 |

In all, 4,115 praise remarks (1,130 of them a bare thumbs up) and 1,646 complaints were counted, out of
6,510 things said (2.01 per review).

**Where the talk goes.** Of the 6,510 things said: game design 35.2% (unlocking alone is 754 remarks,
more than everything said about money); remarks that said nothing specific or were about the review
22.5%; price and how it makes money 8.7% (above most games in the corpus; only Redfall 11.9%, Terminull
Brigade 10.6% and Back 4 Blood 9.5% are higher); community 8.4%; content 5.0%; updates 4.9%; comparisons
4.6%; tech 3.9%; story 3.4%; art 2.9%; sound 0.4%; translation and accessibility 2 remarks each.

### What players praised, most to least
Counts are times it was said (one review can say several things), out of 3,233 reviews. Reviews got much
shorter over the years, so per-100 rates by period mostly fall with them (see How it changed).

1. **It keeps pulling you back** - 221 times (6.8 per 100 reviews); peak 9.5 per 100 in 2016-18.
2. **The price is fair** - 198 times (6.1 per 100). No one complains about the price as such; every
   price remark is "fair". It fell from 8.1 per 100 in 2013-18 to 2.3 in 2024-26.
3. **Plenty of content** - 142 times (4.4 per 100).
4. **Everything can be earned by playing** - 133 times (4.1 per 100).
5. **It looks great** - 128 times (4.0 per 100).
6. **A welcoming community** - 122 times (3.8 per 100), the ninth most common thing said.
7. **The story is worth following** - 119 times (3.7 per 100), against 22 who call it thin or forgettable.
8. **Builds are deep and varied** - 118 times (3.6 per 100).
9. **Updates keep coming** - 117 times (3.6 per 100).
10. **Much better with friends** - 114 times (3.5 per 100).
11. **Combat hits hard** - 113 times (3.5 per 100).
12. **The grind is long and worth it** - 111 times (3.4 per 100). From 2016 on, about one in three who
    raise the grind say it pays off.
13. **Movement rewards practice** - 69 times (2.1 per 100). 17 of the 69 name parkour, bullet jumping or
    the space ninja.
14. **It beats its rivals** - 67 times (2.1 per 100); it rose from 1.5 per 100 in 2013-15 to 3.5 in
    2024-26.
15. **Best free game of its kind** - 62 times (1.9 per 100), all before 2019 (see below).
16. **Each frame plays its own way** - 53 times (1.6 per 100).
17. **A patch made it better** - 51 times (1.6 per 100).
18. **The player market works** - 46 times (1.4 per 100), still said of no other game. Against it: works
    badly 6, scammers 1. *"you can even earn the platinum used to buy things in the market through
    trading in game items with players."*
19. **The developers listen and change things** - 43 times (1.3 per 100).
20. **It works alone** - 42 times (1.3 per 100).
21. **Made with care** - 41 times (1.3 per 100).
22. **A fair business model** - 37 times (1.1 per 100).
23. **The shop sells looks only** - 35 times (1.1 per 100).
24. **Rewarding once learned** - 34 times (1.1 per 100).
25. **You get to feel overpowered** - 34 times.
26. **Players buy things to support the studio** - 30 times (0.93 per 100), the highest rate in the corpus
    (The Anacrusis 0.82, Arcrunner 0.56).
27. **Runs on modest hardware** - 29 times (against 12 who say it needs a strong PC). **Well optimised** -
    17 times.
28. **More to do than fighting** - 23 times, 19 of them from 2019 on.
29. **The premium currency can be earned by playing** - 22 times.
30. **Movement feels responsive** - 14 times; **controls are responsive and clear** - 14 times.
31. **Easy to start** - 15 times. **Welcoming to newcomers** - 13 times.
32. **Cross-save works** - 9 times (against 8 who say there is no cross-save).
33. **The clan hall is worth building** (a shared home a group builds and decorates) - 8 times; only in
    this game.
34. **Rewards teamwork** - 7 times. **Good enemy variety** - 7 times. **Bugs are rare and minor** - 7.
35. **The population is healthy** - 5 times. **Fine with strangers** - 4 times.
36. **Crafting timers help pace play** - 3 times. *"Crafting times help me pace rather than blowing
    through everything in a few months."* (234735996, 704 hours).
37. **Loot is shared** - 2 times.
- Also said, without a verdict: 127 reviews call it "okay", "mid" or "decent" (122 of them thumbs up);
  44 say interest comes and goes in waves - *"You never quit warframe, just take longer breaks"*, in some
  form, appears in three reviews; 72 say it is overwhelming at first; 22 give advice on what not to spend
  on.

### What players complained about, most to least
Counts are times it was said, out of 3,233 reviews. **983 of the 1,646 complaints (59.7%) come from
reviews that still recommend the game.** The median review shows 139 hours (145.5 for the 2,908 thumbs-up
reviews); 1,803 reviews show 100 hours or more, and 555 (17.2%) show 1,000 hours or more. The 325
thumbs-down reviews have a median of 62 hours, and 66 of them show 1,000+. The list is one subject at the
top and a long, thin tail: nothing below the fifth reaches 1.5 per 100.

1. **Too grindy** - 264 times (8.2 per 100). **223 of the 264 are thumbs up (84%).** The 41 thumbs-down
   grind reviews have a median of 194 hours, and 11 show over 1,000 - not new players who bounced. At
   1,131 hours (thumbs up, 200 helpful): very grindy, especially when new, and new content needs a lot
   of resources; *"Money is purely for cosmetics. (Or to reduce the grind out of some stuff)"*. At 6,720
   hours (thumbs down, 456 helpful): *"an extremely heavy grind and a general disrespect to the player's
   time"*, while still praising the visuals. At 1,917 hours (down): *"I shouldn't consider your game a
   damn second job."* At 60 hours (down): *"my progress felt so slow"*. (The pages give no review ids
   for these.)
2. (82 times a thumbs-down review said nothing specific.)
3. **It takes over your life** (a warning) - 74 times in 73 reviews (2.3 per 100); **60 of the 73 are
   thumbs up.** Said of no other game when it was read. *"Relationships are temporary. The Void's
   embrace lasts forever."* (131 helpful). Many are jokes. 9 more say they keep playing while hating it.
4. **Repetitive** - 56 times (1.7 per 100).
5. **Progress held back by real-world timers** - 49 times (1.5 per 100); crafting takes 12 hours to 3
   days.
6. **You need outside guides to understand it** - 43 times (1.3 per 100).
7. **Poorly explained** - 42 times (1.3 per 100).
8. **The part you need may never drop** - 39 times (1.2 per 100). Boosters raise how much you get but
   not the drop odds, so rare stays rare.
9. **The start is slow** - 36 times (1.1 per 100).
10. **Buggy** - 33 times (1.0 per 100).
11. **A mode is not worth playing** - 33 times (1.0 per 100).
12. **Nothing left to chase** - 27 times (0.8 per 100).
13. **A patch made it worse** - 27 times (0.8 per 100).
14. **The story is thin or forgettable** - 22 times (0.7 per 100).
15. **What you can own is capped unless you pay** (inventory slots for frames and weapons) - 21 times
    (0.6 per 100).
16. **Paying affects play** - 16 times (0.5 per 100).
17. **You end up paying sooner or later** - 15 times (0.5 per 100).

20. **Needs a strong PC** - 12 times (0.4 per 100).
21. **You grind for gear you will never use** - 12 times (0.4 per 100).

- **Not on the list at all:** cheating, matchmaking, server trouble as a pattern, or price as such.
- Learning the game, all together: 267 remarks. Overwhelming at first (neutral) 72, outside research 43,
  poorly explained 42, slow start 36, against rewarding once learned 34 and easy to start 15. A reviewer
  at 1,389 hours jokes they are almost done with the tutorial.

### How it changed over time
Reviews shrank over thirteen years, and every per-100 number fell with them. Compare things inside one
period; trust a fall across periods only when it is much bigger than the fall in length. Each period
carries about ±3.7 to ±4.5.

| Period | Reviews read | Thumbs up | Praise per 100 | Complaints per 100 | Mean words per review |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2013-15 | 664 | 90.4% | 116.3 | 57.8 | 67 |
| 2016-18 | 719 | 87.9% | 117.0 | 65.8 | 54 |
| 2019-21 | 720 | 89.6% | 83.6 | 41.2 | 30 |
| 2022-23 | 480 | 91.0% | 65.6 | 42.1 | 30 |
| 2024-26 (to 2026-09) | 650 | 91.4% | 70.0 | 31.8 | 28 |

- **The thumb barely moves in thirteen years**: 87.9% to 91.4% by period; the worst year is 2017 (85.4%),
  the best 2024 and 2026 (92.9%). Praise to complaint holds between 1.6 and 2.2 to 1.
- **Reviews got shorter**: reviews of 15 words or fewer rose from 51% to 69%. The bare thumbs up stays at
  about 35 per 100 in every period, so the shrinking took its length from the specific praise.
- **The grind complaint does not fall** while everything else does: 8.1 per 100 in 2013-15, 9.6, 7.1,
  9.6, then 6.8. It is the only complaint that holds its rate against the shrinking reviews.
- **"The grind is worth it" appears after the early years**: 0.9 per 100 in 2013-15, then 2.7 to 5.0.
- **Repetitive fell for real**: 3.3 per 100 in 2013-15 and 3.1 in 2016-18, then 0.4 to 1.0 from 2019. In
  the same years "more to do than fighting" appears (19 of its 23 from 2019 on).
- **"It looks great" fell the most**: 5.9 to 0.9 per 100, a six-fold fall against a two-and-a-half-fold
  fall in length. The look aged.
- **Learning the game peaked in 2016-18**: outside research 2.6 per 100 and poorly explained 2.9 per 100,
  falling after.
- **A mode not worth playing peaked in 2016-18** (2.2 per 100), and nothing left to chase peaked in the
  same years (1.8 per 100).
- **"It takes over your life" peaked in 2022-23** at 5.2 per 100; many are jokes (*"lost gf bcs of the grind"*, *"like heroin (and not in a good way)"*).
- **"Best free game of its kind" stops after 2018**: 4.1 and 4.9 per 100, then zero in 1,850 later
  reviews, while "beats its rivals" rises from 1.4 to 3.5. The sample cannot say whether players stopped
  saying it or the filing changed between the two reading sessions.
- **Too hard alone** was mostly an early complaint: 9 of the 11 are from before 2019 (1.1 per 100 in
  2013-15, 0.0 from 2022).
- **Other early-heavy complaints:** capped unless you pay 1.4 per 100 in 2013-15, 0.0 to 0.5 after 2018;
  the shop charges too much 0.9 and 0.7 per 100 before 2019, 0.1 or less after; needs a strong PC
  1.2 per 100 in 2013-15, 0.4 or less after; real-world timers 2.0 and 2.1 per 100 before 2019, 0.8 and
  0.9 from 2022.
- **Patches:** "a patch made it worse" was 1.2 per 100 in 2019-23 and 0.3 in 2024-26; "a patch weakened
  what players liked" fell to 0.0 in 2024-26.
- **Praise by period** (read against the shrinking reviews): fair price 8.1, 8.1, 6.7, 4.8, 2.3;
  updates keep coming 6.5 in 2013-15, 1.2 in 2022-23, 2.0 in 2024-26; much better with friends 6.2 in
  2013-15, then 2.1 to 3.8; builds deep and varied 7.8 in 2013-15, then 1.0 to 3.9; the developers listen
  2.2 in 2016-18, 0.2 in 2022-23, 1.5 in 2024-26; the shop sells looks only 2.2 in 2016-18, 0.2 from
  2022.
- No patch notes were pulled, so the pages do not tie turns to named updates.

### Co-op and online play
- **A co-op game that works alone.** Much better with friends 114; works solo 42, against too hard alone
  11 (9 before 2019) and needs a group 9.
- **The co-op praise is about company more than mechanics.** All remarks about co-op design together are
  21, and 3 of those complain that story quests must be played alone. Rewards teamwork 7; loot is shared
  2; more players makes it trivial 1.
- **Hosting (new in the rebuild, small counts, the reviewers' account):** **the host leaving ends
  everyone's run - 4 times**; **peer to peer rather than dedicated servers, so quality depends on whoever
  hosts - 3 times.** Connection trouble: lag and out-of-sync play 4, high latency 3, cannot connect 3,
  frequent disconnects 2, the reviewer's router will not let them host or join 2.
- **Strangers:** fine with strangers 4, poor with strangers 4. The population is healthy 5, but one mode
  has no players 4.
- **Cross-save and crossplay:** cross-save works 9, no cross-save 8; no crossplay at all 1; another
  platform asked for 2.
- **The community is praised far more than blamed.** Welcoming community 122; welcoming to newcomers 13;
  making friends in single figures; the clan hall worth building 8. Against: veterans will not help
  newcomers 3; a much stronger player kills everything first 3; the chat filter bans for harmless words
  6; new players need carrying 12.
- **Teaching is done by other players**, the reviews say: *"ask for help and guidance as often as you can
  early on"*; *"Set your matchmaking to solo for the first few missions"*; *"Don't feel dumb looking up
  plot summaries of missions after you beat them."*
- **Trading between players** is a big part of the game: the player market works 46, works badly 6,
  scammers 1.
- **Not raised as a pattern:** matchmaking, server trouble, cheating. **Bots, friendly fire, joining in
  progress, scaling to player count: nothing said** beyond the single remarks above.

### Combat, movement and feel
- **Combat hits hard** - 113 times; **feeling overpowered** - 34. Against: weightless 1; projectiles too
  slow to hit 1.
- **Movement is praised as a skill to master** - 69 times, 17 naming parkour, bullet jumping or the space
  ninja. Movement responsive 14, unreliable 4. 2 say the levels do not suit the movement.
- **Controls:** responsive and clear 14; the default layout is awkward 5; take hours to get used to 3;
  single remarks on a controller that does not work, rebinding that broke the controls, and the Steam
  overlay shortcut clashing.
- **Camera:** no choice of camera view 2. Nothing else is said about the third-person camera.
- **Each frame plays its own way** - 53; **builds deep and varied** - 118 (7.8 per 100 in 2013-15).
- Overall the game is described through its progression more than its combat.
- The pages say nothing about stuns or how readable enemies are.

### Enemies, bosses and difficulty
- The pages say little here. Too hard alone 11 (mostly before 2019); too easy 8; a much stronger player
  kills everything first 3; new players need carrying 12.
- **Enemies:** good enemy variety 7, lacking 2; one-hit kills 4; poor enemy behaviour 3.
- **Difficulty settings:** harder settings only change the numbers 3.
- **Bosses:** bosses are a chore 2. One grind quote names farming *"the same one type of enemy, boss, or
  mission"*.
- **The end game:** nothing left to chase 27; the end game is only more unlocks 5; you grind for gear you
  will never use 12; the end game demands the best-known build 2.

### Progression, loot, randomness and grind
- **Unlocking is a third of all game-design talk**: 754 remarks (320 praise, 421 complaint), more than
  everything said about money.
- **The grind loop, from four sides:** too grindy 264 (84% thumbs up); worth it 111; paying only shortens
  the grind 73; takes over your life 74 (60 of 73 reviews thumbs up, 82%).
- **Money buys time, not power.** The 73 "paying only shortens the grind" remarks range from boosters that
  raise the amount you get but not the drop odds (thumbs up) to the moment *"you do not want to spend
  another week farming the same one type of enemy, boss, or mission"* and pay (thumbs down) - the same
  fact, a different verdict.
- **Real-world timers are the sharpest edge**: 49 complaints (12 hours to 3 days), against 3 who say the
  timers help pace them (234735996).
- **Everything can be earned** - 133. **Premium currency earnable by playing** - 22.
- **Randomness:** the part you need may never drop 39; boosters do not change the odds.
- **The start:** slow start 36; overwhelming at first 72; needs outside research 43; poorly explained 42;
  rewarding once learned 34. The page: the new-player problem is thirteen years old and named in every
  period.
- **The end:** nothing left to chase 27; only more unlocks 5; gear you will never use 12.
- **Coming back:** returning players get no catch-up 5; the early game left behind 2.
